AccessCode values UNAUTHENTICATED/UNVERIFIED/FORBIDDEN/NO_COMPANY/INVALID_INPUT/CONFLICT/UNAVAILABLE. `src/components/app/auth-pages.tsx` already shows password-changed/revocation-pending; transport rejection uses OUTCOME_UNKNOWN diagnostic/generic failure. Proposed recovery must not tell the user password necessarily stayed unchanged. Workspace cookie may already be committed when refresh fails; current result has no receipt/refreshFailed field. Ready chrome can survive read outages only because operation consumers must independently reauthorize.

Latest recommended **UNAPPROVED** timing/activity candidate from this assessment: preserve2hour server clock, trusted visible pointer/keyboard/wheel/touch and30second coalescing; generic document navigation should validate with touch=false rather than extend idle; focus/visibility/poll/token/background/recovery read only. Touch remains client assertion, not human proof. Existing30second unavailable-check retry is a candidate pending visibility/backoff design.

Logout candidate: immediate captured-session local quarantine+pending+duplicate suppression; **8second soft deadline**, then 'result not confirmed', **30second reconciliation window** with bounded2/5/10second intervals. Never success/server-success/cleanup of another session merely from deadline. Immediate captured-session local quarantine on intent; confirmed session absence/revoke -> scoped cleanup; valid matching captured session -> keep local quarantine and explicit revocation retry only under safe actor/native-session/old-attempt contract; unavailable -> honest UNKNOWN/supportRef. Transport cancellation cannot undo server logout. These replace the earlier5/15 and10/30 draft choices as the current recommended candidate, **not an approved SLO or timing policy**. No repeated mutating retry until safe semantics are established.

Additional unrun definitions: `src/components/app/controls-regressions.test.tsx`, `src/components/app/workspace-switch.test.ts`, `src/components/app/workspace-shell.test.ts`, `src/lib/tenancy/account-profile.test.ts`. Required extra variants fit the20-case manifest: actor switch during pending password/workspace/logout, error/false/malformed/global-logout combinations, navigation recovery not touching idle, committed cookie+failed refresh, stale signals and current revoked authority. These are variants, not new executed tests or an inflated denominator.

### Scope, exclusions and deliverables

Include actual identity/session/idle read versus touch, workspace selection/return, chrome versus fresh authorization, local logout and password-change revocation stages, current RPC wrappers, stale-response/cookie concurrency, safe errors/logging and recovery. Inventory every direct caller/privileged consumer encountered and mark uninspected boundaries. Preserve native Supabase Auth, existing company membership/platform roles/branch grants, account IDs, cookies and established receipts; no new identity/permission engine.

Exclude business creation/payments/subscription/AI/model/provider enablement, SMS/MFA/ownership-transfer implementation, new QA/test projects/pages, production users/policies/settings, blanket log migration, global session deletion, provider messages and Git publication. Branch/Storage/worker/realtime/export/cache scope is mapped and acceptance designed here; actual downstream dispatch/financial/knowledge changes stay in their coordinated slices. No assumption that displaying valid identity authorizes any business operation.

Deliverables within existing Brain only for this planning phase: current signature/result catalog, caller->RPC/data/authority matrix with unresolved entries, effect-stage/recovery state table, compatible old/new wrapper plan, proposed Cursor edit/test scope, Antigravity state requirements and a versioned **20-case unrun acceptance manifest** below. The missing exhaustive private-helper/SQL-consumer denominator must remain explicit before any blanket readiness claim.

### Existing request/result schemas (observed, preserve before extension)

| Existing exact signature / request | Actual result contract or rule |
|---|---|
| checkSessionActivity(touch=false) | Calls authenticated getUser; checkAppSession(client,touch===true). No client timestamp accepted. Returns SessionGuard: {ok:true,expiresAt:string} OR {ok:false,code:UNAUTHENTICATED or UNAVAILABLE,expired?:true} |
| checkAppSession(client:{rpc:unknown},touch=false) | RPC check_app_session args {p_touch:boolean}; parses native {ok,expires_at,code} via sessionGuardFromRpc. Invalid/malformed/errors unavailable except explicitly recognized expiry |
| sessionGuardFromRpc(data,error) | 42501 APP_SESSION_EXPIRED and PGRST301/PGRST303 with JWT expired -> unauthenticated/expired; SESSION_EXPIRED body likewise; invalid expires_at or unknown errors -> unavailable. Full future error inventory still required |
| readSessionIdentity() | Valid getUser+guard -> {ok:true,userId,email,fullName,emailConfirmed,sessionExpiresAt}; false guard otherwise. emailConfirmed currently Boolean(email_confirmed_at OR phone_confirmed_at): do not assume this field proves confirmed email specifically; name/consumer semantics need explicit check, not new identity-policy invention |
| getCompanyAccess(requiredRole?:CompanyRole) | AccessResult {ok:true,context:CompanyContext} OR {ok:false,code:AccessCode,sessionExpired?:true}. ok can contain no company/member; it is not by itself an owner/tenant-operation grant. requiredRole owner rejects differing existing role, not absent membership automatically |
| getWorkspaceSwitcher() | ActionResult data {needsContext:boolean,selectedCompanyId:string|null,isPlatformAdmin:boolean,choices:[{companyId,displayName,role}]}; no actor field in this returned envelope |
| selectWorkspaceCompany(companyId:string,input?:{expectedUserId?:string,next?:string|null}) | Allowed current membership/choice -> HttpOnly userId:companyId cookie, revalidate app/admin, reread access, ActionResult data {companyId,next}. Failure INVALID_INPUT/FORBIDDEN/UNAUTHENTICATED/UNAVAILABLE or access error; post-read request-cache/committed selection semantics require contract check |
| getAccountProfile() | ActionResult<AccountProfileView>; valid native identity may survive company/profile UNAVAILABLE with role/company null, isPlatformAdmin false, accessUnavailable flag. UI identity/chrome ready is not fresh privileged readiness |
| getWorkspaceView() | Parallel switcher+identity, reject company/role disagreement; success data {workspace:switcher.data,identity:AccountProfileView,knowledgeAllowed:boolean}. Preserve request-snapshot evidence; returned switcher lacks actor field, so proposed actor-generation consistency is not claimed already implemented |
| signOut(expectedUserId?:string) | Optional expected-user getUser check; auth.signOut(scope local); only then workspace/return cookie deletion and ActionResult {ok:true,data:undefined}. False result codes UNAVAILABLE/UNAUTHENTICATED/AUTH_ERROR. Cookie deletion failure after native revoke is a possible partial-success stage, not necessarily active session |
| requestPasswordReset(email:string) | Empty input INVALID_INPUT; nonempty provider success/error/exception returns same public {ok:true,data:undefined}, with redacted failure recording. Mailbox-neutral contract preserved; actual delivery not part of this static proof |
| updatePassword(password:string,expectedUserId?:string) | Current min8 validation, native user/expected actor/readSessionIdentity, updateUser({password}), then RPC master_revoke_account_sessions({p_expected_user_id}), then checked global logout. RPC result currently discarded; global failure returns UNAVAILABLE/message PASSWORD_CHANGED_SESSION_REVOCATION_PENDING. Native password may already have changed; never retry password mutation just to recover revoke |

AccountProfileView is the existing type in src/lib/tenancy/access.ts: platformIdentity, optional emailConfirmed/sessionExpiresAt/accessUnavailable/ownedBusinessCount/hasBusinessMemberships, userId/fullName/email/supportCode/isPlatformAdmin/role/companyName/companyId/needsCompanyContext/adminHref/workspaceHref/workHref. Roles/links remain computed/server-authoritative; display metadata and names from user_metadata never grant authorization. Preserve existing types/callers before a versioned change.

### Proposed extensions and effect-stage vocabulary (not current APIs)

- Proposed operation request context: contractVersion, operationId, expectedActorId, **server-bound native session boundary** (not rawJWT/client-provided authority), requested company/branch/channel, required capability, expected membership/ACL revision, client-generation/expected-result version as stale-response hints. Retain present public signatures through reviewed adapters if needed; no generic new endpoint/table presumed.
- Proposed result envelope: version/operationId, authoritative actor-context revision, identity state, authorization state and effect stages separately, safe code/supportRef, recommended next read/re-auth action. Identity state authenticated/guest/expired/unavailable is distinct from authorization allowed/denied/unavailable and outcome not_attempted/pending/confirmed/rejected/UNKNOWN/partial. Detailed enum names are design candidates for approval; client hints do not prove authority.
- Logout effect stages: request bound to actor/native session -> native local revoke outcome -> cookie cleanup -> client/cross-tab cleanup/navigation. Timeout may mean native effect happened; thrown/rejected transport and cleanup error cannot automatically prove no revoke. Confirm old captured-session state through an authoritative read before claiming logout; late old response may not clear a newer actor or same-user new-session context. SDK/global cookie race protection must be verified, not assumed solved by expectedUserId alone.
- Password stages: password mutation result -> database revocation epoch receipt (inspect error/data/false/throw using actual SQL return schema) -> native global logout result -> current caller reauthentication/recovery. Never claim full strict revocation because only one effect succeeded. Preserve password-changed/revocation-pending explicitly; recover revocation without storing/logging/replaying password. 23:41 excludes permissive old-schema fallback: migrate required authority safely; propose and verify account-wide revocation cutoff without weakening checks. [Policy override23:41: actual required server revocation; old-schema missing guarantee requires safe migration, no compatibility bypass. Historical PGRST202 test remains source evidence only.]
- Correlation/recovery receipt contains safe actor/session-bound operation/effect/outcome references only, no password/token/email body/clientsecret. Reuse existing auth/diagnostic/context infrastructure; persistent receipt extension only if necessary after exact native/cookie recovery semantics, not a parallel Auth engine.

### Authority/caller matrix and wrapper compatibility

| Boundary | Current authority / proposed required outcome | Remaining proof |
|---|---|---|
| Proxy/document navigation | proxy -> updateSession -> current claims/guard; safe protected return, expired distinct unavailable | Complete route/caller/request-classification list; no assumption proxy alone authorizes Actions/API |
| Server identity/profile/chrome | readSessionIdentity/getAccountProfile/request-scoped access; valid display can survive company outage without grant | Every consumer must respect absent role/company/accessUnavailable; retained ready chrome never a capability check |
| Workspace selection/read | getCompanyAccess/active membership/expected actor; account-bound HttpOnly hint, safe path and mutually consistent server snapshots | Per-request React cache is not automatically unsafe global caching; verify scope and read-after-cookie behavior. New request must see selected membership or explicit conflict, not fabricate success |
| Actions/API/RPC | requireVerifiedUser/current native check + requested company/branch capability, execute-time grants and target relation | Enumerate privileged/security-definer clients, arguments, current row and direct callers; display context alone insufficient |
| Storage/download/export/realtime/cache/counts | Same active actor/membership/branch/ACL policy; stale context cannot reveal old scope | Exact consumers remain incomplete; do not claim all covered by this task card or a featureID |

| Password/reset/recovery | Native authorized identity, secret input never stored/logged; neutral reset public result; current composed epoch checks | Inspect actual revoke RPC return/permissions/wrapper/native global behavior; no confirmation bypass or SMS enablement |

### Approved23:41 session-security policy and binding contract v3

Source: explicit user message Sentinel_9316fac09d4c81919a1567036c212b8b,2026-10-15 23:41UTC, relayed by parent. APPROVED: actual required server revocation after password change; safe migration when old DB lacks guarantee, no compatibility bypass. Logout connection failure/UNKNOWN blocks local use of captured session, without claiming server revoke. Stale result never changes newer session, including same user. Numeric8/30 timing remains proposed; quarantine starts immediately at logout intent, independent of UX timeout. Never repeat password mutation to recover revoke.

Proposed local transition: ready -> logout_requested_locked -> confirmed_revoked OR locked_unknown/locked_revocation_pending. Captured session remains locked during uncertainty and across recovery; local lock is not server proof. A fresh verified native-session binding can establish a separate new context; old response/reconcile/lock cannot act on it. Preserve safe minimal support/reauth/recovery interface, no cached privileged data/actions under locked session. Read-only reconciliation must be scoped to original operation/session and cannot restore usable ready merely because server still sees the captured session valid. Retry revocation requires original-session-safe authority or explicit reauthentication, never unscoped local SDK call that may target newer credentials.

#### Native-session isolation mechanism and safe migration (supplied complete root-cause summary)

Current source: server-identity.ts omits native session ID; AccountSessionProvider change detection compares user ID, so same-user new native session is not a boundary; performLogout captures/fences user only. Reusable authority already exists in src/lib/billing/wiring/public-purchase-persist.ts: verified getClaims sub/session_id plus checkAppSession, and SQL verified JWT session_id/auth.sessions. Adapt this trusted server path to an opaque sessionBoundary; do not accept client identity as proof or log session tokens. Native token refresh within the same session does NOT increment session generation; verified same-user new login does.

Proposed request/result binding includes opaque sessionBoundary + operationId + client generation. Captured-session revocation/cleanup adapter must be SDK-compatible and independently fence server response cookie writes: JS stale-result checks alone cannot stop delayed Set-Cookie. Direct SDK variants in access-gate, activity tracker and phone-change share this boundary. Old result cannot quarantine/clear/revoke/navigate/overwrite cookies for newer session. Do not delete another legitimate native session or globally clear all browser state to hide the race.

23:41 approved password contract: success only after actual required server revocation confirmed. Missing/false/error/malformed required authority is not success, even if global Auth logout succeeds. Safe compatible migration must install/verify required wrapper/grants/cutoff before activation, with rollback that preserves newer epoch and password state; no bypass for old schema. Password changed + revocation incomplete returns explicit pending stage and recovers revocation WITHOUT changing password again. Historical PGRST202-success test is a migration regression input, not target acceptance or proof of deployed exploit.

Proposed captured-session quarantine persists safely across reload/cross-tab without secrets; lock protected views/actions/autosave/subscriptions immediately. Invalidation signal is not authorization: server-valid alone cannot unlock; recovery/re-auth establishes fresh allowed boundary explicitly. Keep bounded reconciliation/support/reauth only, no cached privileged content. Locks scoped to original boundary must not lock newer session. Exact persistence/SDK adapters to be frozen in WP01A; these are proposals implementing approved outcome, not existing code.

Amend AC01/02/03/10/11/12/13/14/16/17/20 within existing20-case manifest: same-user new login vs refresh, late Set-Cookie, A->B, reload/cross-tab locked persistence, direct SDK callers, server-valid still locally locked, native revoke/cleanup lost, false/missing/malformed authority safe migration, password-changed recovery without credential replay. Each asserts server result, local state, newer-session isolation and redacted evidence; all UNRUN. No timing approval is implied;8/30 remains proposed UX only.

### Reviewable binding, effect and bounded-resource contract v2 (PROPOSED)

Independent challenge source: parent-forwarded two-part static review,2026-10-15; no new source scan or runtime verification by this writer. WP01A is first PLANNING package, not coding-ready. This replaces broad implementation readiness with concrete defaults; existing ActionResult, SessionGuard, CompanyContext, RPC wrappers, receipts and domain stores are reused. New fields/adapters below are proposals, not existing APIs. Installed authority conformance remains Unverified.

Proposed compatible ActionResult data extension: request {contractVersion:1,operationId,expectedActorId,clientGeneration,expectedCompanyId?}; server captures a trusted native-session boundary, never trusts a client session/JWT as authority. Result {operationId,clientGeneration,effect:not_attempted|confirmed|rejected|unknown|partial,stages:{nativeMutation?,authorityRevocation?,cookieCommit?},data?,next:none|read_status|reauthenticate|explicit_retry,supportRef?}. Each stage is confirmed/rejected/pending/unknown where applicable. No token, password or contact payload in receipts/logs. Client generation is only a stale-response fence.

Bind mutation to actor + original native session + operationId; bind UI cleanup additionally to current client generation. Same actor with a new native session is different authority. A late old response cannot clear/revoke the newer context. Before coding establish a trustworthy native-session identifier and SDK/cookie behavior; generation alone cannot prevent a response Set-Cookie overwriting a newer login. Default when target session cannot be proven: no mutation or new-session cleanup, explicit unavailable/conflict. A mutation already submitted is reconciled, not declared cancelled.

| Operation adapter (proposed) | Transaction/effect boundary and truthful result | Recovery/default acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| Existing signOut/performLogout | Capture original native session, recheck actor+session before native local revoke; native revoke and cookie/client cleanup are separate stages, not a distributed transaction | confirmed revoke + cleanup failure = partial; lost response = unknown then authoritative captured-session read, captured local use stays locked throughout. Never revoke a newer same-user session or clear its workspace; no automatic mutating retry |
| Existing selectWorkspaceCompany | Verify actor/session/current membership; commit hint cookie; independent uncached/new-request authority read confirms selected context | cookie committed != context confirmed. Confirmed hint + refresh failure reports committed-hint/context-unconfirmed and read_status; disagreement denies operation authority, no fabricated success |
| Existing updatePassword | Native credential change once -> composed epoch authority -> checked global Auth logout; require actual server revocation;23:41 supersedes permissive PGRST202 success, old DB needs safe migration | PASSWORD_CHANGED_SESSION_REVOCATION_PENDING resumes revocation only, never repeats updateUser(password). Unknown credential outcome does not claim unchanged password. Recovery uses current authorized identity, no stored credential |
| Existing acceptStaffInvitation | RPC membership commit is durable business effect; cookie/navigation are subsequent stages | membership accepted + cookie failure returns committed membership receipt plus recoverable navigation, not generic total failure. Same invitation/recipient replay reuses membership, foreign recipient denied |

Current native topup compatibility constraints: master_apply_ai_topup checks previous_paid_through=applied_paid_through (supports no extension) **and** contract expiry equals cycle expiry/grant inserted at cycle expiry (existing assumption, not approved new validity policy). Reservation currently consumes base then topup then compensation, grant-creation order; this contradicts approved23:13 purchased-first/carry-until-used policy and requires repair. Validate parser/native policy against actual approved catalog versions, never reinterpret500/1500 cost/reply units silently. Wallet/excess collections are money liabilities, not purchased replies.

Proposed end-to-end extension (engineering design only): current owner selects approved immutable SKU/revision with server units/price/currency/terms and explicit target company/branch/service/channel policy; actor operation-key/fingerprint -> dedicated financial purpose through existing order machinery -> Paymob verified processed receipt/reconcile -> once-only recoverable grant via existing locks/source-key -> authorized operational reservation -> actual usage and truthful display/recovery. Do not mutate baseplan or paid-through, don't grant service from browser return, and apply approved purchased-first/carry-until-used policy; do not invent expiry or forfeiture. Unknown collection blocks another charge until reconciled; duplicate successful collections liabilities, not automatic second credit grant. Purchased replies cannot bypass closed channel/AI/review/entitlement/knowledge/budget gates or revive an expired base subscription without separately approved policy. Reply-capacity reserve and model-cost exposure distinct.

ES02/ES04 own finance/order/grant/lifecycle; ES03 consumes approved reply/claim/channel/branch/knowledge/cost/dispatch contracts. Minimum proposed UI fields: selected SKU/revision/units/server price-currency, intended paid scope, no ordinary purchased-credit expiry and rollover until used, subscription paid-through separately, purchased/granted/held/used/remaining, unresolved purchase and stopreason. These are proposed fields, not an existing purchase UI. Purchased-first/no ordinary expiry/rollover until used/separate balances are settled. Only price/bundle/scope/refund/compensation and spending eligibility after base expiration remain unresolved; no balance forfeiture assumed.

Future acceptance variants: callback duplicate/delay/two tabs/key-differentSKU/crash after settlement-before-grant/grant replay/last-credit race/reset-expiry with held-unknown/channel-branch revoke/refund-reversal/double-success; baseplan/version/expiry unchanged before-after. Reload original purchase/grant without recharge. Acceptance must assert purchased-first consumption, no ordinary expiry, rollover until used, separate balances and preservation of owned balance after base expiration. Only price/bundle/scope/refund/compensation and post-base-expiration spending eligibility edge cases await decisions; no real or Sandbox run now. Reuse definitions `src/lib/billing/cycle-credits.test.ts`, `src/lib/billing/cycle-credit-actions.test.ts`, `src/lib/billing/cycle-native-finance.postgres.test.ts` and existing Paymob concurrency/settlement suites. No definitions executed.

Purchased FIRST across cycles, no ordinary expiry, reset base only; held/UNKNOWN retains original grant, release returns same grant, accepted settles once. Inactive base still displays owned balance; spending eligibility unresolved, no forfeiture or permission invented |
| Paid setup/review FC03+FC04 ES03 | {operationId,expectedRevision,fieldPatch} or {submissionRevision,fileGenerations,reviewAction} -> {committedRevision,reviewReceipt,approvedRevision,readinessReasons,refreshState} | CAS existing save attempt; exact immutable generation approval; changed content cannot inherit old review. Durable invitation acceptance and saved setting remain committed when refresh fails; recover by authoritative read, no duplicate mutation |
| Dispatch/cost FC05+FC06 ES03 | {logicalIntentId,attemptId,leaseFence,controlRevision,contentHash} -> {providerOutcome,persistenceOutcome,accountingOutcome,providerRef?,next} | Provider accepted is sticky evidence even if finalize fails/throws: preserve sendAccepted:true, do not claim delivered, never automatic resend. Reconcile same intent/usage receipt; final authority check before HTTP, issued HTTP not retractable. Inspected route uses sendAccepted only for accepted count; no resend authorization from false. Existing ready-only claims/accepted repair retained; duplicate sends not proved |
| Revoke/deletion/restore FC08+FC10 ES01 contract/ES04 execution | {operationId,scope,expectedRevision,intent} -> {blockedReasons,committedStages,pendingStages,next} | Commit grant change/tombstone and receipt; fence future work, not claim recall of issued URL/HTTP. Legal hold blocks destruction. Restore Auth+SQL+Storage manifest and immutable effects/tombstones before reopening, outbound OFF during reconciliation; backup snapshot is not restore proof |

Business-sensitive choices only: topup prices/bundles/scope/refund/compensation/post-base spending; shared-outage formula, grace restrictions, bonus anti-abuse proof, model spend/paid evaluation, crossbranch sharing, legal retention/holds and recovery loss/downtime budgets. Technical defaults above are proposed for review, not an indefinite questionnaire or reapproval of settled policy.

Source: same authorized Astra continuation, chunks1-5 of5 received; J01-J36 below, structural journey format only. J references are audit/acceptance rows, NOT new tasks/UXplan or certification of every JSX control. E=existing source entry/control, P=proposed required contract, M=no complete implementation established in bounded map; E never Verified. Roles S subscriber/O owner/E employee/M branchmanager/A authorizedVeloraadmin/C endcustomer. Role labels are not permission. All acceptance UNRUN, no invented coveragepercentage. Each row inherits U1-U8, N/A must be explained in later manifest. Approved policies above prevail over proposed field/interface names.

Universal contract applied to EVERY J row:
- U1 Entry/authority: actor/native-session generation/company/branch/targetID+revision/capability/entitlement revalidated on deep link and effect; C channel identity+verified customer association, no forcedVeloraaccount. Navigation grants nothing.
- U2 Fields: label/required-optional/format-limit/help/localized field+summaryerrors, firstinvalidfocus; preserve allowed input only, NEVER password/PIN/token. Unpaidprep page-memory-only/no restore/serverdraft; paid persistence only authorized.
- U3 States: initial/loading->empty|available|blockedreason; mutation pending->confirmed|partial|unknown|rejected; unavailable != empty/denied/expired/zerousage. Exact committed effect named.
- U4 Recovery: one operation per unchanged intent/doublesubmit suppression; UNKNOWN status before fresh effect; committed+refreshfailed retains receipt/saved-latestviewunavailable. Closingview != cancellingservereffect.
- U5 Change: stale/deleted target reload or safe list, no silentretarget. Revokedgrant/newsession disables affecteddata/actions; old async cannot revive. Crosstabhints invalidate, never grant.
- U6 Exit: safe authorized filters/location/allowedpaidinput; reload owned durable op not assumedfailure; foreign/deleted deeplink no leak; denied user authorized account/workspace exit not only inaccessibleSettings.
- U7 A11y/device: keyboard/screenreader/status withoutalertstorms/focusreturn/localizedRTL+bidi/reflowzoom/mobilekeyboard/safearea; no hoveronly/coloronly state. All actual behavior untested.
- U8 Acceptance per row: role/scope+valid/invalid/empty/readfail/denied-revoked/stale-deleted/pendingduplicate/partialUNKNOWN/reloaddeeplink/crosstabsession/recovery, AR/EN mobiledesktop; explicitN/A when genuine. No current runtime or allcontrol denominator.

#### Durable WhatsApp send: existing intent, result-loss cause and reconciliation adapter

Source: complete parent-supplied root-cause summary2026-10-15, replacing interrupted partial transfers. Existing durable pre-send intent ALREADY exists: whatsapp_stage_job_reply20261008132000_vel_wa_worker_atomic_integration.sql:166-190 keys wa-job:<jobId> and binds original job/body/conversation/channel-link generation/AI attempt/citations/reserved usage. whatsapp_claim_assistant_job_send:257-279 persists ready->attempting, attempt token/start/expiry BEFORE HTTP; later current-authority wrappers retained. send.ts:133-170 one POST reports accepted only with provider message ID, never delivery.

Cause: src/lib/whatsapp/integration-store.ts reduces rich finalize_whatsapp_send_attempt result to boolean; integration-worker.ts:63-67 maps known provider accepted + false finalize to blocked/sendAccepted:false; outer catch75-77 similarly drops known acceptance on throw. inbound-worker forwards and internal route only increments accepted count; false does not authorize resend in inspected callers. Existing-intent repair/ready-only claim/accepted receipt compatibility controls remain valuable. Source defect is inaccurate acceptance result/API undercount, not demonstrated duplicate-send exploit; runtime recovery still Unverified.

Proposed existing-adapter result (NOT new messaging engine): {intentId,attemptRef,provider:{outcome:not_attempted|rejected|accepted|unknown,messageId?},receiptPersistence:confirmed|pending|unknown,timelinePersistence:confirmed|pending|unknown,accounting:confirmed|pending|unknown,reconciliation:none|read_original|retry_finalize|hold_manual,supportRef?}. Preserve logical job/intent, attempt/provider correlation and original usage; accepted is sticky evidence independent of local persistence. No accepted->rejected downgrade from database timeout, no accepted==delivered label.

| Failure boundary | Safe transition/recovery in ES03/PF06/PF07/PF10 (proposed) | Future acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| Known accepted/message ID; finalize false/throws | Return provider accepted/message ID, pending receipt/timeline/accounting stages; read original intent and retry FINALIZATION ONLY with same attempt/provider ID | False/throw/lost-finalize-response leaves sendAccepted:true, no second transport/model generation, once-only original usage/timeline |
| Finalization committed but response lost | Read original receipt/state; reconcile remaining timeline/accounting stages using original keys, no new intent | Replay/crash converges on committed receipt with no duplicate timeline/credit/cost effect |
| Crash after possible HTTP / provider response unavailable | UNKNOWN, retain held capacity/original attempting intent; lease expiry or missing local receipt is not evidence of nonacceptance | Worker restart/stale lease cannot blindly resend/regenerate or free uncertain capacity |
| Exact-correlated receipt/callback available | Verify native supported provider evidence and exact intent/attempt/message scope, then persist accepted and finalize accounting/display | Replayed/foreign/unmatched callback cannot settle wrong intent; accepted receipt repairs same effect |
| Provider outcome cannot be recovered | Provider lookup capability is NOT proved; hold UNKNOWN with assigned owner/manual resolution and explicit authorized disposition | No fabricated lookup, false failure/success or automated resend; unresolved exposure and customer/operator state truthful |

Repair belongs existing ES03 dispatch/reconciliation package; ES04 adds operator follow-through, not prerequisite for minimum safe ownership/recovery. Preserve no-blind-resend controls; no provider calls/tests performed.

Stale approval/key-changed-intent/replayed operator action/revoked worker/field conflict deny or reconcile without duplicate effect. Numeric thresholds/rate caps/SLO owner choices pending, scheduler activation not authorized.

#### Proposed voice control/media separation and lifecycle

Durable control plane reuses existing Postgres authority/receipts/leased jobs, adds scoped adapters only. Realtime media gateway separately terminates authenticated carrier/provider streams, bounded jitter/audio/VAD/bargein/session tool orchestration. NO audio chunks over DB job queue; no default new distributed infrastructure. Hosted directSIP can avoid custom media hop; custom gateway justified only by chosen path and future measurements. Keep provider and carrier IDs/capabilities separate. Client never holds provider secrets or arbitrary dial authority.

Proposed control states: requested -> policy_validated -> budget_reserved -> queued/ready -> originating or inbound_accepted -> ringing -> connected -> ending -> ended; independent blocked/rejected/cancel_requested/unknown/reconciliation_needed. Provider accepted != human answered != resolved business outcome; call ended != cost settled/record deleted. One callIntent may have multiple explicitly authorized legs/attempts; reconnect is not new dial or replay of tool effects. Unknown after possible submission never blind redial; expired lease/missing receipt no proof call absent. Automatic retries only proven not-submitted/rejected and purpose/country/customer policy allows. Cancellation ack separated from confirmed termination.

| PROPOSED data contract, new names not existing tables | Minimum scope/authority/receipt/transaction rule |
|---|---|
| voice_call_intents | id/company/branch/direction/purpose/customerRef/contactRef/capability/entitlement/actor-nativeSession+generation/consentRef/policyknowledgepricingversions/opkey+intenthash/state/controlRevision; dialnormalized server after validation, no model-selected tenant/number |
| voice_call_attempts + voice_call_legs | intent/attempt/leg/provider+carrier/account/environment/externalCallRef/from-toRefs/leasefence/requesttime/accept-ring-answer-end evidence; UNIQUEprovideraccount+externalid and logical attempt. Transfer legs separate billable effects |
| voice_call_events | authenticated webhook/eventID/attemptref/sequence-time/hash/safe payloadref/processedreceipt; durable inbox dedup then reducer, duplicate/out-of-order status cannot regress answered/ended fact |
| voice_consent_records + voice_action_receipts | purpose/country/callpermission/notice/recordingterms/revision/source/expiry/revoke; typed approved tool target+argHash/current tenantbranchgrant+consent checked EACH effect. Immutable tool op receipt prevents reconnect replay |
| voice_spend_reservations + voice_usage_evidence | call+leg/provider/model/carrier/tariffrevision/currency/maxminutes+currency/concurrency/heldunknown/incurredestimatedconfirmed/CDRref; use existing finance writer, no duplicate spend ledger. Reservation units defined, not borrowed replyunits |
| voice_media_artifacts + voice_reconciliation_receipts | recording/transcript/objectimmutablegeneration/access/retentionlegalhold/deleteDue/providerdeleteproof; cost/state reconcile and human owner/nextattempt/closure refs; source and derived copies inventoried |

All data tenant/branch scoped, current capability at read/decision/effect; inbound number/asset->verified tenantbranch binding before accepting media, claimed phone number alone not customer ownership. Service-role/telephony callback adapter explicitly scopes target, not broad bypass. RLS/grants/Storage/export/recording/download same rule, caller/recipient redacted in general logs. No default crossbranch knowledge or callrecord sharing. Existing strict-session/authority contracts apply to human console and new actions, not provider webhook session impersonation.

| PROPOSED API/job/event adapter, new endpoint names NOT actual | Input/result/recovery contract |
|---|---|
| POST /api/voice/intents (outbound), POST /api/voice/inbound/decision | opkey/purpose/verifiedrecipient-consent/scope+version or authenticated inboundproviderref -> callIntent/policystate/budgetreceipt/next; validate current authority, supportedregionmethod/timewindow, no clientamount/numberfrommodel |
| GET /api/voice/intents/:id, POST .../:id/terminate, POST .../:id/handoff | owned currentcapability+expectedrevision -> separate provider/carrier/media/cost/handoff stages; closeUI!=terminatecall, knownendedfact sticky; humantransfer requested!=accepted!=answered |
| POST /api/voice/webhooks/:adapter | vendor-specific signature/auth/replay/account/env and exactcall binding, persistdurableevent thenack; no generic Stripe signature assumption. Eventidentity separate business-effect uniqueness |
| internal voice_control_jobs | originate/accept-policy/terminate/handoff/reconcile-call/settle-usage/delete-artifact/notify; existing leasedjobpattern, sameintent/attempt/fence/nextretry/deadline+owner, no mediaframes in durablepayload |
| realtime media adapter | short-lived scoped call session, verifiedstreamorigin/callref, boundedframes/VAD/bargein, current controlrevision/tool policy; droppedstream -> reconnect original session only when supported, no reenact tool/charge/call |
| typed voice action gateway | explicit allowed businessaction schema, current branch/capability/targetrevision/consent/spend/approval where required; untrusted transcript/model cannot authorize money/order/stock/identity or choose company; confirm consequential action, receipt returned |

Initial transaction locks callscope/budget and inserts intent+reservation+unique command receipt BEFORE external action; do provider HTTP outside DB transaction. Reconcile exact native supported lookup/callback/CDR, no guessed capability. Known accepted with failed persistence preserves externalref/outcome, repair same receipt only. Crash after possible external action -> UNKNOWN held spend and assigned owner, not redial. Human handoff checks actual eligible target/currentshift and provider route; answer acknowledgement distinct from successful transfer; failed/unavailable human has approved fallback/notice/termination, not endless on-hold. Already issued audio/HTTP cannot be recalled; revocation stops future effect/frames where supported and terminates safely.

Spend PROPOSED: reserve conservative per-call upper bound across AI audio/text/history/tools/transcription+carrier rounding/minimum/transferlegs/recording/infra, limits pertenant/branch/call/concurrency/day; incremental metering renews bounded reserve before next slice. Hard cap triggers truthful notice/stop/handoff with bounded termination latency; provider lag/rounding may add cost, never promise zero overshoot. Interrupted/unknown calls and handoffextra legs may still bill. Retain uncertain exposure, settle actual provider usage+carrier CDR later, compare prices/units/currency/FX source and dispute anomalies; do not erase incurred model/carrier cost if customerreply becomes ineligible. Customer billing/sale price/minuteplans/refund remains unapproved; purchased TEXTreply credits separate from VOICEminutes, no silent unit conversion/free pass.

Recording/retention PROPOSED: purpose/country-required notice+consent+recording configuration evaluated before call; unsupported mandatory recording/consent capability BLOCKS that use case. Default record-off only where lawful/contract permits; cannot override UAE marketing requirement. Versioned retention for recording/transcript/toollogs/derived summaries/provider/carrier/backup, legalholds and generation-safe deletion receipt; UI private authorized playback/download with auditable access. Provider delete scheduled != confirmed erased, ownDBdelete != provider/carrier delete. Exact countryrecording retention/legal review required, not chosen by vendor default.

### Session and workspace seams - first-phase inputs

- `src/lib/auth/server-identity.ts`, `src/lib/auth/session-guard.ts`, `src/lib/tenancy/access.ts`, `src/lib/supabase/middleware.ts` contain native server identity/session checks.
- `src/lib/tenancy/actions.ts:274` checks expected user, calls auth.signOut(scope local), then deletes workspace/return cookies. `src/components/app/account-chrome.tsx:209` clears/navigates after success; CURRENT SOURCE transport rejection retains identity for retry;23:41 TARGET supersedes usable retention with immediate captured-session quarantine across protected views/actions/autosave/subscriptions/reload/cross-tab, without claiming server revoke. No bounded logout deadline found; a stalled promise can leave pending UI indefinitely. Deadline/outcome semantics are a proposed WP01 contract, not an approved arbitrary timeout.
- `supabase/migrations/20261008010000_vel_session_idle_policy.sql` defines two-hour activity/native-session/pre-request/restrictive-policy foundations. `supabase/migrations/20261014033007_master_password_session_revocation.sql` adds native expiry/banned/deleted checks and account revocation epochs. Installed parity/settings uninspected.
- session-activity-tracker.tsx throttles trusted visible interactions; middleware also counts document navigation. Authenticated client can call checkSessionActivity(true). Browser isTrusted is a UI filter, not server proof of a human. Exact intended inactivity semantics need explicit acceptance before claiming conformance.
- access.ts accepts userId:companyId cookie hints only after membership checks; selectWorkspaceCompany validates membership and sets HttpOnly cookie. session-context.ts storage hints do not grant access; account chrome fences old responses, workspace-view.ts rejects disagreeing identity/workspace reads.
- Still unverified: real multi-workspace owner/staff transitions, stale tabs, revoked memberships, expired sessions, Storage/direct RPC and branch-sensitive final provider dispatch.

## Shared contracts

- **IDENT-001:** user_id = native auth.users.id, never editable email/phone/metadata. Require verified identity, valid native session_id and active server membership; expected user/company fencing prevents late-account results. Broadcast/storage signals invalidate only; recovery cannot silently merge accounts.
- **TENANT-001:** persisted business key = public.companies.id / company_id / TS companyId. Product business_id refers to this identity; do not invent a second key or choose first company. Workspace picker/server revalidation supports multiple businesses per account.
- **BRANCH-001:** company_branches.id / branch_id belongs to company via composite (company_id,id) FK. Enterprise employee identities/account bindings are explicit. Null/primary branch is not blanket access; company-level inbox/billing does not establish branch isolation.
- **ROLE-001:** company owner/staff, enterprise manager/employee and internal platform teams are separate authority systems. Platform admin cannot substitute customer membership. Invitation identity/company/branch/role/revision and current session are checked at action/dispatch boundaries.
- **WRITE-001:** CAS profile/branch/channel revisions plus durable actor-bound attempt IDs; identical retries reuse receipts, different payload conflicts. Late responses/cancelled generations/account switches cannot overwrite newer state or delete replacement Storage objects.
- **FIN-001:** immutable plan version -> server quote/order snapshot -> merchant/environment/amount-bound evidence -> proven settlement/application -> grant -> trusted activation/cycle. Preserve company/environment lock order; no duplicate ledgers/guessed backfills/display-derived entitlement.
- **CHAN-001:** paid plan entitlement, selected channel revision, native provider connection/permission and account/role/branch operation are four independent facts. Every worker/final sender rechecks current authority and connection; payment alone is not readiness.
- **MSG-001:** conversation/message/job/send-intent/company/recipient/link-generation bindings stay immutable. Reconcile unknown provider outcome before retry; retain window/takeover/final-dispatch rules across reconnect/renewal.
- **KNOW-001:** approval/retrieval pin company/source/file generation/hash/version. Replacement/invalidation removes stale authority. Review approval never activates payment, Meta or AI.
- **USAGE-001:** reservations/credit/cost/reply capacity bind proven company subscription cycle; expiry/reversal/replay cannot extend time or mint grants. Tariff estimates differ from final provider cost; merchant accounting is not Velora billing accounting.
- **FLAGS-001:** Smart/Master/Enterprise/smart-review/provider/send/payment gates are separate; smart-rollout.ts covers presentation/setup writes only. Revalidate exact schema/config before activation; no broad flag bypass.

### Lifecycle and actual navigation

`src/app/app/layout.tsx:7` authenticates, verifies email/profile and requires company/context; it does NOT check payment or operational readiness. No-company users go to `/setup`; context-selection users may render children. Platform admin without company/context goes to `/admin`. Therefore intended post-subscription design context is a target/use case, NOT evidence of a current paid-route guard. Visitor/auth, blank prepayment preparation, existing-company setup/recovery and operational workspace remain distinct.

`src/app/setup/page.tsx:22`: authenticated no-company users get PrepaymentPreparation (no server draft restoration); company-present users get SetupWizard, with masterJourneyEnabled and smartOnboardingEnabled passed to that child. Company presence here is not proof of paid provisioning. This route is distinct from `/app/settings`; do not impose workspace navigation on blank preparation or infer all onboarding navigation is rejected.

`src/components/app/workspace-shell.tsx:144` owner sidebar, exact AR / EN labels and routes:

|---|---|---|

### Reachable settings and exact business controls

`src/components/app/settings.tsx:65` defaults to account. Explicit business screen is `/app/settings?tab=business`. Owner tabs: حساب المستخدم / Your account; معلومات النشاط / Business information; الفريق والدعوات / Team and invitations; واتساب والتفعيل / WhatsApp and activation; الباقة والاستخدام / Plan and usage. Staff only account. Non-owner requested owner tab produces locked notice; owner plus initialCompany mounts BusinessTab. No branches tab. These are distinct child families with existing UXC mappings, not fields in the business form.

|---|---|---|

Save button is disabled if unchanged/name blank/conflicts, busy state provided; conditional conflict button adopts latest conflicting values only. There is no ordinary discard button, email input, country selector, business-type selector, reply-language selector, branch add/rename or branch/team tab in this form. fromCompany preserves business_type/reply_language/country/contact_email in form state and resubmits them unchanged. They are not visible read-only controls either. contact_email is a business contact, distinct from signed-in account email shown above the tabs. normalizeBusinessEmail allows unset, trims, max254 and validates non-whitespace mailbox syntax/control characters; legacy hidden invalid values may block save. Current persistence uses these companies columns; do not promote them into canonical target branch/company/account ownership without the approved domain contract. No manager/branch-scoped editing authority is proved by this owner form.

### Save path, SQL authority and recovery

settings.tsx:250 -> src/lib/tenancy/actions.ts:915 saveOwnerBusiness -> getCompanyAccess; rejects staff membership, missing context and platform-only no-company; checks expected user/company and runtime field types. It normalizes contacts and validates name/type/country. Current-company version present requires safe expectedVersion >=1 and calls update_owner_company_profile(expected user/company/version, eight-key patch). masterJourney enabled + missing version returns UNAVAILABLE. Legacy versionless branch calls upsert_selected_owner_company (or upsert_owner_company when no company), not a proven CAS path.

supabase/migrations/20261014015934_master_business_authority.sql:93-116 is source evidence, NOT proof deployed: SECURITY DEFINER with empty search_path, explicit auth.uid/native session/verified identity/active owner membership and non-suspended company checks. Locks companies FOR UPDATE and membership FOR SHARE; expected version mismatch -> CONFLICT. Validates eight-key payload via private.master_business_payload / valid_company_setup_payload. Updates display_name,business_type,reply_language,city,hours,contact_phone,contact_email,updated_at; version trigger increments version. Appends private.company_profile_write_audit(company_id,actor_id,action,previous_version,new_version). Country difference writes private.company_sensitive_change_requests(company_id,actor_id,expected_version,field,old_value,new_value), returns REVIEW_REQUIRED, leaves profile unchanged: a real separate committed effect despite unsuccessful profile result. No country editor/approval panel exists in this BusinessTab.

Migration enables RLS and revokes client table privileges for private audit/request/attempt tables; revokes PUBLIC/anon RPC execute, grants authenticated execute, revokes direct companies updates/old owner APIs. Definer explicit authority is material; RLS alone does not establish safety. Native-session helper checks auth.sessions and banned/deleted users. Compatibility fallback may fail after those revocations; installed migration/schema/composition still unverified. No claim all later SQL definitions were audited.

Server known committed receipt stays ok when revalidatePath fails (REFRESH_FAILED warning). Client replaces baseline from receipt and catches router.refresh separately. Concurrent refresh reconciles untouched fields and flags conflicts; conflict adopt button does not retry writes. UI maps all failed results to generic authFailed rather than actionable validation/conflict/review-specific messages. Client fences by current user only, not native session/operation generation. No save operation ID/receipt lookup here: transport error can be UNKNOWN after commit; retain draft, re-read authoritative company/version and reconcile before a deliberate new save, never claim rollback/no-effect or blindly retry. This is proposed recovery guidance, not existing complete UI behavior. Draft guard covers company switch and browser unload; tab router.replace does not invoke it, while BusinessTab stays mounted hidden.

SSR0.12.7 createServerClient forces cookie storage despite custom auth.storage; lazy getSession/getUser/getClaims may refresh; listed auth events persist set/remove changes through setAll. createBrowserClient likewise uses cookies, defaults browserauto-refresh/singleton; passing storage does not replace its cookie authority. cookies.ts chunk/remove/apply pipeline has no cross-response generation CAS. Admin.signOut(jwt,scope) POST/logout?scope has no cookie storage removal and no cutoff/session-list parameter. VERIFIED WRITER MAP extension: src/lib/supabase/server.ts:setAll; middleware.ts:updateSession:setAll/refresh, src/proxy.ts copying responsecookies; app/auth/callback/route.ts pendingCookies+cookieStore.set+finish responsecookies/exchangeCodeForSession; tenancy/actions.ts server signInWithPassword/verifyOtp/updateUser/signOut/workspacehint; tenancy/personal-phone-actions.ts updateUser/verifyOtp; personal-phone-change-actions.ts verifyOtp/local/others; browser factory direct imports in access-gate.tsx/account-chrome.tsx/auth/browser.ts/session-activity-tracker.tsx. components/app/auth-pages.tsx ALSO has DYNAMIC browserfactory use for login/getUser; literalimport scan alone missed it, now explicitly included. Workspace/return/PKCE cookies can also confuse laterflow, so bind ancillarystate to sameflow/generation; no globalcleanup of B's returnpath.

BR01 RECOMMENDATION / SINGLE DECISION: authorize scope extension from shared-cookie adapters to a minimal Velora server session broker with generation-isolated opaque handle cookies. Keep Supabase Auth identity/native sessions/JWT/RLS, current roles/business rules. Browser holds opaque handle only; access/refresh tokens encrypted server-side. Use plain supabase-js with server-owned storage/request-bound credential and existing JWT-RLS, NOT SSR createServerClient customstorage (ignored). No genericpermissionengine or providerlogic rebuild. Rich client calls existing ServerActions/read routes; browser Auth observer becomes broker identity status, no direct Supabaseauth refresh/login/logout/token persistence. Public anonymous nonauth functionality unaffected; future directbrowser DB/realtime token consumers need explicitly owned broker-compatible authorization before enablement, not automatic service-role bypass.

COOKIE PROTOCOL (proposed new contract, names illustrative not existingfiles): each committed native context issues random highentropy opaque handle under UNIQUE per-generation host-only cookie name, Secure/HttpOnly/Path=/, suitable SameSite+CSRF/origin checks. Name contains no rawnativeID/token. Request may carry multiple generation handles; server verifies hashes/registry and chooses latest eligible authoritative auth-intent ordinal, NOT request cookie order/client counter. No shared current-session/pointer cookie is overwritten or cleared on login/logout/refresh; fixed-name BFFcookie alone would reproduce BR01. Authintent is created/reserved before providerlogin/callback with serverordinal, trusted expectedparentcontext, expiry+PKCE/state nonce; late oldintent cannotbe promoted above a newercommitted intent via latercallback arrival. First unauthenticated flows have independent expiring perflowcookies/serverstate, no sharedbootstrap cookie that lateA canoverwriteB. Multiple legitimate concurrent account logins are resolved by server intent ordering/parentCAS; intent with staleparent rejects, notlast-response-wins. Registry selection validates native/epoch/admission/lock, never promotes an older residualhandle when newercommitted context is revoked/quarantined (tombstone/no-fallback rule). Cookie evicted/missing/forged ->reauth/failclosed, no privilege from ordinal/name. Cookie count/header limits require boundedoldhandle cleanup; cleanup only exactenumerated oldnames, never prefixclear/newBnames. No timebased retention value invented.

Refresh updates encrypted tokens for SAME native session under registry rowrevision/CAS+per-session serialized refresh, emits NO authhandlecookie; currentSDK refresh reuse rules preserved, no disable-reusedetection. OriginalAlogout/status/cleanup writes NO sharedauthcookie; if cleanup of cookie needed expire only A's unique name; it cannotdelete B. Even stale legacy response sets old sb-* cookies, broker ignores them afteradmissioncutover; no dual-reader fallback to legacy. Ancillary workspace/return/PKCE state serverbound tohandle/flow, not shareduser-only cookie. OldAaccount actions capture nativehandle/generation+scope and compareagain at effectboundary; stale resultdrop stillrequired, notcookieguarantee substitute. Appregistry credentials encrypted, requestclients never singleton userstate, lock/rotation/expiry/CSRF/OAuth/statuslimits and minimalrecovery authorized; new schema/module names assigned onlyimplementation.

GUARANTEE LIMIT: under protocol+completewriter migration, delayed response A cannot overwrite/delete B's handle or activate A over B. No guarantee browser refuses all oldcookies, no XSS/hostileorigin/takenoverbrowser protection, no alreadyissued bearerURL instantaneousrevocation, no completed pastdomaintransaction rollback, no globalR06coverage claim. Browser cookieeviction/network/registryoutage may force reaush/failclosed availability; not permission recovery. Historical unswitched clients/tabs must be denied at cutover, not supported concurrently via legacyreaders. Actual reversedheaders test NotRun, required later; recommendation is reasoned design, not Verified implementation.

BR02 CURRENT CUTOFF: master_revoke_account_sessions true writes n=clock_timestamp and greatest(revoked_before,n), inserts/expires activity for native sessions created_at<=n. Wrappers allow only native sessions created_at>cutoff plus identity/nativechecks. This immediately denies apppaths honoring wrappers; tokenrefresh of SAME oldsession doesnot change created_at. It does NOT itself remove native auth.sessions/refresh tokens, affect every externalJWTconsumer or guarantee no preauthorizedeffect finishes. Retry currently recomputes n and mayinvalidate newerB; caller selfrevoked mayreturnfalse. Thus existingRPC not retry-safe originaloperation and BOOLEAN is only apprevocationstage, not nativeAuthcompletion.

BR02 SELECTED MECHANISM for broker-managed sessions: reserve durable revocation operation and account admission barrier BEFORE credential mutation; after confirmed credential write choose cutoff ONCE at first revocation commit, snapshot target native sessions and frozen pre-barrier auth intents, then neveradvance cutoff duringresume. Bind encryptedtargetcredentials serveronly. Ordinarylogout originaltargetone. Passwordallrequiredsessions snapshot allknownnative targets atcutover, EACH cleanup via stateless server auth.admin.signOut(targetJWT,'local'), no cookie-boundclient.signOut, no global/others recovery. For expired capturedJWT, renew ONLY same capturednative session from its serverstored refresh credential forcleanup ifproviderallows; verify returnedsub/session_id equals target, neveradmitrenewedoldsession toapp orreplaceB. Rotation storedCAS; ifcannotauthorize ->nativepending notfalseok. Provider204 accepted stage and authoritative absence of exactsession via narrowserver-only scopedstatus/probe distinguish confirmednativeabsence vsJWTerror/unknown; 401/403/404 notautomaticallyconfirmedlogout. No directauthsessionsmutation, no manufacturedJWT, no userID-onlyserviceoperation. Statusauthorization minimaloriginalop recovery, receipt afterselfrevoke, no passwordreplay. NativeJWT may remain cryptographicallyvalid, protectedappdenial independent.

SUPPORTED API BOUNDARY / LEGACY: pinnedAdminAPI accepts a valid targetJWT+local/global/others ONLY, no revoke-by-nativeID-list or cutoff parameter found. Therefore an unregistered legacydevice with no capturedcredential cannot be honestly nativelyrevoked by the selected per-session API merelyfrom DBrowID. Do NOT invent such endpoint or useglobalretry. Migration inventories registry/native session coverage; legacyuncovered targets remain appdenied/pending-native, fullpasswordrevocationSUCCESS BLOCKED until supported native cleanup/confirmedabsence. Managedregistry must cover all legitimate appnative sessions before claiming universal per-session cleanup; no silentdrop of olderdevices. Optional one-time provider-global legacycutover would need separatelyapproved maintenance/admission exclusion AND confirmed upstream outcome before newadmissions; UNKNOWN keepsbarrier, not autoretry/unlock. It is NOT chosen as normalrecovery and not presentlyauthorized. Exact hostedAuthversion/sessioncapabilities remains deploymentfact, not excuse for genericresearch. No configuration to disablepublicAuth, revokeallusers or directDBdelete proposed.

DECISION TO USER (ONE architecture scope): approve or reject broker+generation-isolated handles and legacyadmission/cutover safety scope for WP01A. Cost: encrypted servercredential/session registry, centralizedAuthIO+refresh locking, CSRF/OAuth adaptation, allnamedwriters migrated, deliberate legacyreauth/compatibility gate, serveravailabilitydependency and cookiecountbudget. Benefit: concrete enforcement of approved new-session isolation withoutbrowserimpossibleCAS, supported originalsession cleanup for brokerknownsessions. No newbusinessrule/Meta/permissionengine. If rejected, existing sharedcookieadapters can only provide appauthorization/staleresult safety+detect/reconcile cookie damage, NOT original strictneveralterB cookieguarantee; that would require explicitchange toapproved guarantee, not silentacceptance. We recommend approvearchitectural scope; programming remains unauthorized. Legacyunknownnativecleanup cannot be declared solved by scopeapproval; it is explicit migrationpreflight/acceptance blocker.

### WP01A-C11 password linearization and late login intent clarification

Original operation is reserved BEFORE credential mutation; cutoff is NOT an arbitrary early request timestamp. Broker admission barrier serializes newauth-intent admission for the account, preserving other users. First confirmed credentialwrite ->first apprevocation transaction fixes cutoff and native target snapshot ONCE. Frozen pre-barrier login intents are also targets by originalintentidentity: their late-issued native session is never admitted and is locallycleaned when its exactcredential arrives, not confused with a newpost-barrierB. Unknown credential/auth-intent outcomes remain pending, no passwordreplay/globalretry. Revocationresume cannot add unrelatedpost-barrierintents or move cutoff. A legitimate B admitted after committedbarrier release is outside originaltarget set, survives alloldoperation cleanup; concurrent session captured inside intentional accountwidecutover is an explicit password-security target, not accidental stale cleanup. Explain this semantic distinction in acceptance, notclaim every session evernewerthanrequeststart survivesaccountwidepasswordrevocation. Ordinarylogout remains exactlyonecapturednative target. Nativecleanupsuccess requires exacttargetreceipts/absence, olderunregisteredlegacy remainspending asC8. This clarifies chosen brokercontract without selecting a newcommercialpolicy or authorizingcode.

R2 SQL vel_broker_admit_session:121-127 tombstones EVERY other active native session of p_user_id, so a legitimate login on device2 invalidates device1. registry.ts maxActiveAuthOrdinalForUser:234-248 and resolve.ts:143-153 also compare across all devices. Meanwhile switching to another user does not fence previous user's A, so missing B cookie restores A (S01). FIX explicit authoritative browser/device/intent lineage, preserve independent sessions on other devices and account scope for password revocation only; retain durable lineage fence after top-cookie loss/logout/expiry. ACCEPT same-browser same-user and cross-user A->B never restores A; independent device A remains active after B ordinary login; actual browser cookie-loss/reversed-response tests. P06 old-state counterexample is limited as documented, not proof of fresh single-user path failure.

C3-02 [P1, V02,V03,V10,V15] — Reject stale parent/intent and recover atomic admission outcome. R2 SQL:91-100 compares intent_parent only to supplied p_parent_handle_hash, does not validate authoritative current lineage/parent or any newer committed intent. An old reserved ordinal1 can finish after B ordinal2, tombstone B at :121-127, then insert old A ordinal1. login actions.ts:281 and startOAuthAction broker-actions.ts:22 do not derive/pass parent; OAuth exchange login.ts:168 falls back to the intent's own saved parent, making equality tautological. Same-ordinal/legacy0 ambiguity checks in resolver do not repair late old-intent admission. FIX atomic current-parent/committed-order CAS in proper lineage, include anonymous first-intent race contract; idempotent receipt for committed intent/insert when RPC response is UNKNOWN (currently committed retry rejected as INTENT_STALE, not reloaded). ACCEPT reverse completion leaves B active and A rejected/frozen, repeated original admission recovers same receipt without new native/cookie mutation, concurrent barrier fixture proves atomicity. Do not replace this with user-global invalidation.

C3-03 [P1, V07,V09,V10] — Preserve legacy pending through recovery. registry.ts:403-442 returns legacyCoveragePending=true; password.ts:276-303 correctly blocks full success and keeps barrier, BUT this coverage state is not frozen in operation. recovery.ts:245-271 comment claims coverage pending but conditional checks only managed pending/empty; with all managed receipts native_confirmed it releases barrier and returns full success (S02). FIX durable coverage status/proof in original operation and identical completion predicate in original/resume/ack; only actual authorized native coverage/absence can resolve pending. Include account-wide tombstoned broker natives in coverage disposition: C3-01 currently leaves their native tokens active while active+quarantined inventory excludes tombstones. ACCEPT legacy+managed, quarantine/tombstone, empty/partial/DB-failure cases cannot become confirmed on resume, new B after original cutoff survives, no credential replay/cutoff advance.

C3-04 [P1, V06,V07,V09,V10,V17] — Separate cleanup capability from mutation authority; make self-revoke recovery reachable. resolveBrokerSessionByFingerprint resolve.ts:201-228 returns any-status captured row/token without active/app-session attestation. Recovery actor is now derived from cookie possession (improvement), but broker-actions.ts:118-120 requires original handle cookie that logout/password paths expire; barrier then blocks fresh login, so original recovery can become unreachable. Password actions.ts:525 uses same cleanup resolver and :533 checks a separate readSessionIdentity without comparing its user/boundary to the resolved target; stale/tombstoned A must not gain password authority from current B. FIX distinct active authoritative mutation resolver vs bounded original-operation recovery capability, safe durable receipt usable after cookie expiry/self-revoke, trusted owner/boundary checks and no client actor claim. ACCEPT foreign user/device/operation denial, original recovery after cookie loss, app-revoked A denied mutation even when B is valid, no token/receipt authority escalation. Credential UNKNOWN still needs explicit non-replay recovery/operator disposition, not perpetual unexplained barrier. No unauthorized-access exploit claimed from static inspection alone.

C3-05 [P1, V01,V03,V10,V11] — Lease must precede native auth mutation, not just persistence. sync.ts:35-52 acquires lease after receiving already-mutated session; phone actions.ts:107 (verifyOtp call) through :120-124 invokes sync AFTER provider response. Concurrent refresh can rotate during that provider operation before sync ever claims. New-SID branch sync.ts:60-64 bypasses same-session lease using admission, with parent validation gap C3-02. UNKNOWN refresh correctly fences replay (P10), but no explicit uncertain-rotation reconciliation path established; crash can leave rotating forever. FIX shared operation serialization/fencing around actual provider mutation and token write, safe UNKNOWN/crash disposition, not time-based blind steal. ACCEPT slow/concurrent refresh+OTP/update paths, native SID mismatch, CAS loss, process crash; deterministic recovery or explicit reauth without blind rotation. No SMS/provider test allowed until safe approved fixture.

C3-06 [P1/P2, V02,V04,V05,V11,V14,V20] — Complete UI boundary/idle contract. account-chrome.tsx expire:98-106 now retains userIdRef and boundary, but tracker.tsx:18-19 immediately returns when hasCurrentUser() true; hasCurrentUser at account-chrome.tsx:300 tests retained userIdRef. Profile :135-149 only compares userId across two independent status/profile requests, not native generation for same-user B. auth-pages.tsx:722 reads captured session.boundaryFingerprint after await (stale render object) and live isCurrentUser only fences userId, not same-user generation. AccessGate's :45 property comparison likewise uses captured session object; ensure all result fences read live refs. ACCEPT exact2h expiry, retained A capture, new B during awaits, transport throw/outage, storage failure, no ready/protected flash while locked. Do not change approved idle/touch timings.

C3-07 [P1 safety, V15,V16,V17 + DB01-DB18] — Make fixtures safe and keep original acceptance semantics. wp01a-db-contract.test.ts:12-14 silently falls back to SUPABASE_DB_URL; skipReason:28-33 only rejects two hosted substrings, accepts arbitrary remote hosts/S03. C3-08 [P1, V02,V03,V11,V20] — Require browser-bound OAuth intent. callback route.ts:75-79 explicitly accepts preferredState when cookieIntent is null (`|| !cookieIntent`), so matching browser intent cookie is optional (S04). Server-held PKCE verifier does not itself prove initiating browser ownership when code+intent URL can be moved to another browser. FIX require exact intended per-intent cookie/browser binding, fail closed for missing/mismatched cookies without expiring siblings; enforce parent/ordinal from C3-02. C5-01 [P1; V02,V03,V05,V10] Shared vel_bl reintroduces delayed-cookie authority race; first-root CAS incomplete. cookies.ts ensureBrowserLineage:117-130 generates/writes one UUID per empty request, so concurrent initial requests create different roots (S01); commitBrowserLineageCookie:134-136/admit.ts:62-64 and login.ts:130-131 overwrite shared vel_bl even for delayed A. resolve.ts:118-125 trusts pointer A to ignore present B, and :126-134 accepts a single valid remaining lineage when pointer is missing, so A can return after B/pointer loss. SQL C4:140-141 obtains tip only with parent; no-parent but same lineage never loads tip_ord, and locks admission by user only (:109), not common lineage. Two cross-user roots/same-lineage parentless attempts can commit out of order; :202-207 tombstones current B even when old ordinal A arrives late. intent_lineage and supplied lineage are coalesced :163 without mismatch rejection. FIX one authoritative server-bound browser attempt lineage/root/version with correct initial bootstrap/serialization, immutable committed order, no shared mutable pointer activation from late responses; missing/tampered pointer cannot select older A. Lock/CAS lineage for parentless roots and validate intent/parent/lineage binding and new ordinal vs tip. Keep independent devices. ACCEPT actual anonymous first-login race, crossuser/sameuser/tab/device, stable/missing/tampered vel_bl, reversed Set-Cookie and B-loss/expiry; B survives and other devices unaffected. This is original no-A-resurrection acceptance applied to new pointer mechanism, not a moved goalpost.

C5-02 [P1; V01,V11,V20] New required lineage not supplied by signup/email verification. admit.ts:32-37 now rejects absent browserLineageId BEFORE registry; tenancy/actions.ts:229 signUpWithEmail and :458 email verify still call admitNativeSession({session,writer}) with no lineage. C5-03 [P1; V02,V03,V07,V09,V15,V17] Receipt replay validity/cryptographic context incomplete. Normal new receipt now encrypts handle via registry.ts:185-203 (fixed at-rest new path), but SQL C4:80-102 returns committed receipt BEFORE expiry/barrier/current-status/tip/lineage checks; checks user/native only, not receipt_lineage vs caller, or row still authoritative. ### One consolidated C5-R3 correction package (Cursor code/test definitions only)

**Browser-init concurrency contract (same browser; devices independent):**
- S1 No `vel_ba`: server may issue distinct genesis attempts; only highest `issue_seq` may stick in cookie.
- S2 Late response with lower/equal `issue_seq` must not rewrite `vel_ba` (keep cookie winner).
- S3 Equal-seq different attemptId: keep cookie; do not thrash.
- S4 Resolve requires v2 attempt lineage match; orphan A after B won cookie cannot be selected by demoting cookie.
- S5 Cross-device roots remain independent (no shared pointer cookie).
- Failure codes: `BROWSER_ATTEMPT_STALE_RESPONSE`, `BROWSER_ATTEMPT_EQUAL_SEQ`, `BROWSER_ATTEMPT_STALE`, `HANDLE_LINEAGE_MIXED`, `LINEAGE_FENCE_NO_MATCH`.

R3-01 [P1] Init protocol in `browser-attempt.ts` + resolve fence; no new lineage pointer cookie.

R3-04 [P1] Honest provider cleanup outcomes; all admit-fail exits.
R3-05 [P1] Probe columns/signatures + client requires R3 flags.
R3-06 [P1] DB01/DB18b/migrations list. Legacy no-AAD decrypt removed (fail closed).
