## WP01A C5-R4 chosen protocol and executable Cursor handoff — 2026-10-16

Status: **PROPOSED design / NOT implemented / NOT runtime verified**. This section supersedes R3 claims that a server CookieReader reread prevents response rollback. Scope PF01 (+PF10) / ES01-S01 / WP01A only. Owner explicitly requested root-cause investigation and a concrete Cursor implementation task. Preserve commit a442e34 and all later dirty work; no reset, unrelated rewrite, install, credentials/configuration, migration execution, Production, provider/SMS/paid calls, cleanup DROP, commit/push, next flow. Cursor changes application code and test definitions only; Codex independently executes authorized verification. This document is the complete handoff, not a claim of delivery to Cursor UI.

### Evidence and causal finding

Current observed HEAD a442e34; R3 source and Brain edits remain uncommitted. Targeted reads traced auth-pages -> tenancy actions / browser.startOAuth -> broker-actions -> login/admit/registry, auth callback, proxy/middleware, account-chrome, password/recovery, sync and direct phone callers. No credential file inspected. Dependencies checked afresh: node_modules Vitest, TypeScript, Next, pg and @playwright/test entrypoints all absent. Process broker key and three fixture settings absent (presence only). No local DB/project/account proof exists from this check; no connection attempted. Node v24.18.0 is available.

Actual source proof artifact outside app: C:/Users/DELL/Documents/Codex/2026-10-16/task/wp01a-protocol-proof.cjs and wp01a-protocol-proof-results.json. Run by clean child process with only OS PATH/SystemRoot/TEMP/TMP + CI and existing offline-network-guard. Exit0, 11 assertions: 3 characterize the current counterexample; 8 check the chosen abstract protocol/completion. The first assertion executes the actual stripped ensureBrowserAttempt function with synthetic signing/DB/request stubs, no real key. Both originally empty request readers emit Set-Cookie. A separate HTTP delivery model applies B then A and leaves A. Remaining checks model coordinator/server generation/completion, not real Web Locks, browser, SQL, SDK, Auth or RLS. Not P/S/V/DB acceptance and not production proof.

Root cause: browser-attempt.ts ensureBrowserAttempt:111/119/143 reads the same request-scoped reader, then writer.set:155. broker-actions.ts:119-125 and tenancy/actions.ts:468-475 close over next/headers cookies for that request. Browser B response does not mutate A request. Retained R2 issue RPC:35-42 still reuses valid A after independently issued B; :45-47 supersedes A only if presented on a mismatched/nonreuse path. :50-54 creates unrelated genesis roots. A signature/sequence establishes authenticity/order of issuance, not shared browser identity or supersession. R3 DB18b:494-502 explicitly expects A reuse; wrong-sequence +999 at :503-515 is not a late valid A token.

Primary references verified: https://www.w3.org/TR/web-locks/ (cooperative exclusive lock scoped to a storage bucket; callback lifetime and agent termination); https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/Web_Locks_API (secure contexts, broadly available since March2022, runtime feature detection still mandatory); https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc6265.html section4.1.1/4.1.2 (concurrent Set-Cookie race/replacement). No claim that Web Locks authenticates a caller or provides cookie CAS.

### Chosen architecture: stable container continuity + server authority generation

Choose ONE protocol: exclusive Web Lock + persistent continuity anchor; immutable server binding; server-reserved per-lineage generation and exact committed tip. Reject cookie compare patches, localStorage check-then-set without a lock, IP fingerprints, latest sequence across users/devices, or calling two same-partition tabs separate devices. An IDB-only coordinator is technically possible but adds a second implementation path; do not implement that fallback in this task. Unsupported/denied Web Locks or storage fails closed before provider calls.

1. Canonical first-party origin only. Same origin/storage partition/profile tabs share a continuity identity. Separate browsers/profiles/private partitions/devices are independent. Reject embedded/third-party initialization. Do not derive canonical origin from untrusted Host/forwarded headers; reuse validated project site URL configuration. Cross-origin aliases redirect to canonical before Auth state initialization.
2. New client module browser-continuity.ts owns navigator.locks.request('velora:wp01a:continuity:v1', {mode:'exclusive'}, callback). Inside callback reread localStorage key velora:wp01a:continuity:v1, validate schema, generate a 32-byte crypto.getRandomValues anchor ONLY for genuinely empty enrollment, persist and read-back equality BEFORE any mirror/broker request. Record version and anchor; no password/JWT/handle. Never use steal, a lease timeout, BroadcastChannel leader election, or lock held over provider/OAuth navigation. Network timeout does not erase/replace anchor. AbortSignal may cancel queued acquisition only; once granted callback finishes normally. A dead tab releases the lock, but the already persisted anchor lets next tab retry the SAME enrollment.
3. Client sets request-visible host-only mirror vel_bc from that persisted anchor, Secure on HTTPS, SameSite=Lax, Path=/, no Domain. This mirror is JS-readable, never an Auth credential, never set from URL/query/storage messages, never written by server Set-Cookie. No response may clear/replace it after login/logout. Same-partition tabs serialize initialization and mirror restoration. BroadcastChannel/storage events are hints to refresh/lock UI only, never authority.
4. Before creating a fresh anchor, call same-origin no-store continuity-status under the lock. It returns only whether identity artifacts exist (broker handles, v1/v2 attempt, per-intent cookies, reset receipt); no identities/tokens. Missing anchor with surviving mirror/server-visible artifacts -> CONTINUITY_RECOVERY_REQUIRED, no new root or provider effect. Existing valid anchor + missing mirror -> restore same mirror under lock. Anchor/mirror conflict -> lock UI and explicit recovery; do not choose larger sequence. Malformed anchor/storage write/read failure -> CONTINUITY_UNAVAILABLE. Both truly absent/artifact-free -> create. No silent new root on parser failure or env/DB outage.
5. POST /auth/broker/continuity accepts the anchor only in same-origin request body paired with the mirror; exact Origin + Sec-Fetch-Site policy and existing CSRF/ServerAction validation. Rate-limit enrollment. Server hashes anchor and invokes service-only bind RPC. Unique hash => same immutable lineage for all retries/late replies. Response contains protocol version and lineage fingerprint/state, never session tokens. Endpoint never sets vel_bc or a per-login shared pointer. The high-entropy anchor is sensitive continuity metadata (redact it), NOT proof of a user. Only verified Supabase identity/native session plus authorized intent can admit; possessing anchor alone cannot obtain JWT, handle, identity or RLS authority. XSS/shared compromised storage is outside the coordination guarantee; preserve XSS/CSP/cookie protections. Reject foreign-origin fixation attempts; never accept client userId as identity.
6. Enrollment validity: keep immutable binding through login/logout/idle expiry. Use existing 30-day cookie retention for mirror; server enrollment valid_until is extended to 30 days after successful fresh provider admission, never by navigation/touch/bootstrap retry. Expiry does not generate a new anchor/root: app resolve locks, fresh Auth attempt on SAME enrolled root is permitted and, after provider proof, advances generation/renews enrollment. Intent TTL stays existing600s. No DB pruning/deletion in this task. Explicit reset closes old root and invalidates its current authority under server lock BEFORE replacing anchor; requires freshly verified owner if old identity exists. Unknown reset result => keep old anchor/reset nonce and reconcile, never create another. Missing anchor with artifacts has no automatic recovery in this slice: show existing Notice with explicit clear-site-data-and-fresh-login recovery; never silently recover old session. If ALL browser continuity/cookies are erased, server cannot identify that physical browser; new explicit Auth creates a new root, old handles/callbacks cannot be promoted. State this limit in tests and docs. No auth timeout/2h/touch change.
7. Old vel_ba v1/v2, legacy sb-* and vel_bl never create or select a new root. Existing installations require explicit reauth/cutover into coordinated continuity; do not merge unrelated legacy roots or assume migrations ran. Old roots remain denied/retained until separately approved cleanup.

### Durable generation/admission contract

Reuse generation-named opaque vel_sh_* cookies, AES-GCM AAD, Supabase JWT/user validation, RLS, intent cookie+PKCE, revocation guards and refresh fences. Add public.vel_broker_browser_lineages: id uuid PK, bootstrap_hash text UNIQUE NOT NULL, latest_reserved_generation bigint NOT NULL default0, current_generation bigint NOT NULL default0, current_registry_id uuid nullable, state active/closed, valid_until timestamptz, created_at/updated_at. RLS on; no PUBLIC/anon/authenticated table access; service_role minimal CRUD; sequence privileges explicit. Anchor hash is grouping only, never JWT authority. Keep browser_attempts historical, fail-close its issuing RPC for new consumers.

Extend operations with revision bigint NOT NULL default0 and completion_receipt jsonb nullable (actor,operation,immutable cutoff,target snapshot/receipt digest,completed_at,barrier release key); validate receipt schema before idempotent historical success. Extend auth_intents with lineage_generation bigint, request_nonce uuid and UNIQUE(browser_lineage_id, request_nonce), provider_state ready/in_flight/session_captured/unknown/rejected, cleanup_state not_needed/pending/confirmed/unknown/rejected. Encrypted provider snapshot refs and verified user/native fields are server-only. Create/claim attempt durably BEFORE provider call; request_nonce retry returns same attempt/state, never allocates fresh generation or replays provider after in_flight/unknown. A post-provider crash before credential receipt is genuinely UNKNOWN; supported provider/session inventory reconciliation is required, not an exactly-once claim.

Service-only SQL RPCs, SECURITY DEFINER, empty search_path, fully qualified objects, explicit revoke PUBLIC/anon/authenticated and service grants:
- vel_broker_bind_browser_continuity(p_bootstrap_hash text) returns jsonb. Insert first-wins immutable binding by unique hash, return existing same root; closed returns CLOSED, no reassignment.
- vel_broker_reserve_bound_intent(p_bootstrap_hash text,p_request_nonce uuid,p_purpose text,p_recovery_operation_key text default null,p_ttl_seconds integer default600) returns jsonb. Lock root first, idempotent nonce lookup; allocate generation by root counter +1, save parent/current capture/purpose/recovery, server state_nonce. Latest reserved attempt is eligible; a failed/cancelled new attempt never makes an older pending attempt eligible again. Existing committed session is not revoked merely by reservation.
- vel_broker_claim_provider_attempt(p_intent_id uuid) returns jsonb. One ready->in_flight CAS; repeat returns minimal status, no second provider call. Callback/broker caller must supply request root+intent proof before claiming. OAuth start may set verifier later via conditional update; callback claim guards exchangeCodeForSession, do not claim provider exchange at OAuth redirect generation.
- vel_broker_record_provider_session(p_intent_id uuid,p_user_id uuid,p_native_session_id uuid,p_access_token_enc text,p_refresh_token_enc text,p_token_expires_at timestamptz) returns boolean. Bind only verified provider identity/SID; store encrypted refs and session_captured/cleanup pending. No plaintext token fields in JSON DTO/logs.
- vel_broker_admit_bound_session(p_intent_id uuid,p_user_id uuid,p_native_session_id uuid,p_handle_hash text,p_cookie_name text,p_generation text,p_access_token_enc text,p_refresh_token_enc text,p_token_expires_at timestamptz,p_admission_scope text,p_recovery_operation_key text) returns jsonb. Root/generation/parent come from intent, never caller-selected root. Check identity, TTL, encrypted receipt/AAD contract, captured session, current eligible generation, barrier owner/purpose/scope, root state and parent. Under root+user lock update intent+registry+tombstones+exact current_registry_id/current_generation+receipt atomically. Committed retry returns exact receipt only if still current/allowed; otherwise minimal STALE status with NO handle activation. Recovery requires matching open/pending password operation/owner and active matching barrier; app cannot use recovery scope. Service-only direct callers must use same checks. Replace old14 and 10/12/13 and retained commit_intent_ordinal unsafe side channels with explicit deprecated denial, no DROP. Update every in-tree caller first.
- vel_broker_resolve_bound_session(p_bootstrap_hash text,p_handle_hashes text[],p_admission_scope text default'app') returns jsonb server-only. Load requested root, exact current_registry_id/current_generation and that active row; require its exact handle hash/name present. Missing B => UNAUTHENTICATED even if A active/tombstoned cookie remains. Resolve requires hash AND generation AND cookie_name equal the exact server current row (including scope/native identity); mere matching user is insufficient. Current tombstone is an authority fence; never use max(active) as fallback. Ignore unrelated old-root cookies for selection or return explicit conflict; never select them. Validate scope/expiry and native user agreement; return encrypted refs only to trusted server, no browser/PostgREST role. Do not cache authority across mutation.
- vel_broker_close_browser_continuity(p_bootstrap_hash text,p_reset_nonce uuid,p_expected_registry_id uuid) returns jsonb. Verified owner/captured current authority required before call when bound identity exists; terminal close/reset receipt idempotent, preserve newer-root/device state. No native deletion/legacy cleanup side effect. Reauth route can renew expired same root rather than close it.

No server response writes or expires vel_bc, including error/callback/logout/reset responses. Reset client updates mirror only after durable close receipt, under the same lock; other tabs reread persisted anchor under lock, never stale in-memory anchor. Global lock discipline: root advisory lock(s, UUID-sorted only when genuinely multiple) -> user admission row(s, UUID-sorted) -> operation row -> intent row -> registry rows. Never acquire root after user/operation/intent lock. Move initial intent FOR UPDATE in old admit (R3:221-228) after root lookup/nonlocking read then revalidate locked row, so intent cannot introduce a hidden reverse edge. Completion needs user->operation only, never subsequently root; revocation RPC holding operation must not later request admission/root. Refresh holds registry only and never subsequently root/user. Inventory/snapshot must not acquire roots while holding user; freeze after user barrier using stable records and revalidate contracts. Audit retained RPC bodies/callers for this order; fail-close unused unsafe compatibility entrypoints rather than assume they are unreachable. Test two independent connections with deterministic barriers, not sleeps.

### Revocation completion and durable cleanup

Keep signature vel_broker_complete_revocation_operation(uuid,text), but replace body. Add vel_broker_patch_operation(p_operation_key text,p_expected_revision bigint,p_patch jsonb) returns jsonb for nonterminal allowlisted updates; eliminate direct unguarded status patches. Terminal retry returns current minimal receipt without applying patch. Lock user admission then matching actor operation. Validate kind/actor. If already confirmed, return immutable historical receipt success WITHOUT changing ANY current barrier, including a newer barrier. For pending, validate all BEFORE writes: currently active barrier belongs to this operation (or same operation release receipt proves previous release); immutable cutoff and credential_mutated_at valid/equal recorded confirmation boundary; credentialMutation/appRevocation/nativeRevocation confirmed; snapshot_frozen true; legacy absent/resolved plus recorded resolution evidence where required; nonempty snapshot with valid unique registry IDs/native identities belonging to actor; every frozen target has durable native_confirmed receipt; no unexpected/malformed target substitution. Validate cleanup/scope obligations separately rather than counting recovery reauth as an old cutoff target. Then one transaction confirms operation and clears only own barrier. All false returns precede writes. Existing zero-snapshot completeness rule retained. Confirmed terminal enforced by DB trigger/CAS on operation updates (freeze status/cutoff/snapshot/target receipts/stages; allow only monotonic client-cleanup acknowledgement and updated_at); late failure handlers cannot downgrade it.

Introduce vel_broker_record_target_outcome(p_operation_key text,p_registry_id uuid,p_outcome text) returns boolean: user->operation->registry locks, actor/frozen target checks, merge ONE target receipt (avoid whole-map lost updates), native_confirmed terminal, registry revoked/quarantined disposition written atomically with receipt. No operation may claim completeness from an in-memory map. Password/recovery re-read durable receipts then complete; on completion timeout/unknown, re-read/reinvoke SAME RPC/status, never update confirmed to pending. If no recovery cookie after completion, allow fresh app Auth to read own minimal historical receipt; do not require active recovery barrier to acknowledge already-completed operation. Never replay password or advance cutoff. All stage/status helper writes conditional on expected revision and nonterminal state; confirmed idempotently returns current receipt.

Provider journal: reserve/claim intent BEFORE all signIn/signup/verify/exchange/reauth provider effects. Immediately capture verified native result encrypted before branching or writing cookies. On EVERY failure after provider-success (lineage, reserve, identity mismatch, storage, admission, cookie writer throw, caught exception, stale generation), one server finally path, registered immediately when provider session becomes available and disabled only by durable admitted/current success, records cleanup obligation and invokes signOutProviderSessionAfterAdmitFailure. Return typed {outcome,cleanupOperationId}; confirmed only on provider evidence. Unknown/rejected gets durable encrypted target refs/outcome and retryable cleanup status; never just log+forget. If DB receipt write is unavailable, retain already-created pre-effect journal as UNKNOWN, attempt cleanup in-memory, truthfully block with the same operation ID; later provider inventory/manual authorized reconciliation is required if snapshot was lost. Do not invent guaranteed recovery of a native SID that was never durably observed. No provider APIs added without documented support; no raw refs in browser state. Recovery reauth new session is recovery-only and has its OWN cleanup/disposition journal, not inserted into original frozen cutoff targets. A new native SID from phone sync is likewise journaled/admitted under pre-reserved bound intent; same-SID token refresh retains existing lease/CAS path.

### Unified precise correction handoff (Cursor code and real test definitions only)

R4-F01 [P1 migration/runtime blocker; R4-C1,V15,V16]. R4 SQL 603-606 replaces vel_broker_complete_revocation_operation(uuid,text) RETURNS jsonb, but R3 SQL 78-81 defines SAME signature RETURNS boolean. PostgreSQL CREATE OR REPLACE cannot change return type (official https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createfunction.html Description). In the supplied sequential migration chain this aborts the transaction; not an executed DB failure. Preserve the existing boolean return signature/OID and expose the minimal receipt through existing status reader, or introduce a differently named v2 JSON RPC with explicit caller/probe updates; choose boolean preservation for this correction, no DROP. R4 patch_operation:494-504 also unconditionally references support_ref, a column absent in the inspected migration chain; its first UPDATE will fail even when patch lacks support_ref. Remove that unsupported field from allowlist/UPDATE (no caller needs it), or add an explicitly justified schema field before use. Existing legacy_coverage is TEXT with pending/absent/resolved check (C3 SQL:18-21), but R4 complete declares legacy jsonb:617 and assigns op.legacy_coverage:669; ordinary values are not JSON and lead to conversion failure. patch_operation:503 assigns p_patch->'legacy_coverage' JSON into that text column. Keep the established TEXT enum and use ->> with strict validation. Qualify/verify gen_random_bytes at reserve:152 with empty search_path; extension namespace is an environment integration question, not assume public/extensions. Add sequential clean-chain + R3 upgrade + reapply tests before any actual migration request.

R4-F02 [P1 durable completion contract; R4-R1/R2/R3]. ### Small consolidated residual correction — do not reopen closed fixes

1. **P1 SQL lock inversion remains in claim, not the corrected admission.** R4 SQL claim_provider_attempt:287 locks intent, then root row:294-295. reserve locks root then inserts/reads intents; admit holds root:424-427, user:435, then intent:439. A duplicate claim on a captured intent can hold I and wait R while admit holds R and waits I; PostgreSQL may abort one, leaving uncertain auth. This is a source-proven lock cycle, not an executed DB deadlock. Fix claim with a NONLOCKING initial intent->root lookup, acquire root advisory/root row first, then lock/revalidate intent and check eligibility/state. Keep same public signature/claimed semantics; no need new API for this fix. Add deterministic two-connection claim-vs-admit and claim-vs-reserve tests, no sleeps.

2. **P1 committed OAuth receipt lost its intent expiry check.** registry.materializeCommittedIntentAdmission:742-795 no longer checks intent.expiresAt, and SQL admit committed branch:441-473 returns before expires_at check:479. P03 actually materializes an expired intent using a valid current-row RPC stub. Restore finite/not-expired intent guard before decrypt/cookie activation in materialize; enforce same condition in SQL committed replay. DO NOT apply the600s intent TTL to ordinary session resolve (that would incorrectly expire normal sessions after10minutes). Keep regular session refresh/2h policy unchanged. Pin expired committed callback -> no cookie/provider call, current valid lost-reply retry -> original receipt.

3. **P1 cleanup path still incomplete under exceptions/unknowns.** verifyEmailCode calls admitNativeSession:582-589 inside outer try, but catch:619-621 owns no captured session/finally; writer/capture/admit throw after provider success returns without cleanup. Signup now has that catch ownership and should be reused, not rewritten. provider-session.persistCleanupState:58 excludes committed intent while login/OAuth catch can call signOut after committed admission+cookie-write throw; this can revoke native session yet leave current registry/cleanup disposition unreconciled. Native cleanup still uses setSession:93-100, which may refresh before signout; existing captured-token admin signout avoids this extra mutation. Implement one captured-session cleanup/finally in verify/newSID throws, reconcile committed cookie uncertainty before revoking, and propagate durable:false/pending ID to callers. A scan of auth code found no reconcileProviderCleanup implementation/claim_owner; do not claim cleanup is automatically resumed merely because unknown refs were stored. Add an owned cleanup reconciliation entry consuming captured encrypted refs or explicitly retain truthful blocked/manual status. Pending/retry UX currently calls takeAuthGestureNonce each ensure:206/215/245; peek is not used to preserve UNKNOWN gesture across a new attempt. Preserve same-nonce transport safety and add a concrete UNKNOWN resume/retry test before claiming complete idempotent UX.

4. **P2 durable completion edges, no boolean redesign required.** patch_operation allows snapshot_frozen=false:669-670 while snapshot remains unchanged; trigger checks only changed target_snapshot when old frozen:1029-1030. Two calls can unfreeze then rewrite the frozen target set. Reject true->false in RPC AND trigger, and keep frozen cutoff/legacy protection. Client cleanup ACK recovery.ts:370-381 attempts stages.clientCleanup=confirmed, but updateBrokerOperation returns true without writing any confirmed operation; trigger also forbids all stages changes. Add narrow monotonic clientCleanup acknowledgement path that writes only that field and cannot alter native receipts/status/cutoff; test stored ACK, not just true. Completion also inserts an admission row:823-825 before rejecting unknown/foreign operation, contrary to byte-equivalent false contract; lock existing admission and reject absent row without insertion (a pending own active barrier must already have a row). No new business rules needed.

5. **P1 executable test defects still prevent meaningful acceptance.** wp01a-continuity-protocol.spec.ts R4-A1:70-93 falls back to GET status, never invokes the coordinator, and only compares anchors IF both exist; null/null can pass. R4-A2:108-118 intercepts REQUEST continuation, performs no login A/B and asserts only typeof page.route; this is not reversed Set-Cookie response proof. R4-A3 only checks independent localStorage, A4 checks a string it just wrote. Replace with actual existing Auth UI actions, mandatory nonnull anchor/root assertions, two pages concurrently in one context, route.fetch/fulfill response delay, missingB resolve and independent-device assertions; use bundled Playwright+installed Chrome as prepared, no @playwright/test install required. Removing expect.fail was not sufficient. DB10:327-334 supplies13 args but expects DEPRECATED_USE_BOUND_ADMIT (only14arg body returns this;13arg returns DEPRECATED_SIGNATURE). Correct expected code for13arg and separately test14arg, preserving both denied. DB10 still stops after claim with no provider capture/bound admit: add actual chain. DB18b:489-506 directly inserts resolver fixtures/pointer; keep it as resolver behavior test but add real reserve/claim/capture/admit and overlap test for admission rather than calling manual insert 'admit A then B'. Complete positive/repeat/newer-barrier fixture at DB18b-r4:565-618 is now useful; preserve it.

### Proposed bounded implementation package - NOT EXECUTED

1. Preserve each developer's local environment file. Before any Git mutation record safe metadata/permissions, verify ignore and plan review of only the intended index delta. Proposed command git rm --cached -- .env.local removes the index entry only; do not delete/rename/overwrite the working file, use git clean, reset or broad add. Revoke/replace the exposed OpenAI credential once for its aliases; treat Gemini, Meta app secret/challenge token and Supabase elevated credential as needing provider-specific containment unless owner establishes they were already safely invalidated. For legacy Supabase service_role JWT, determine supported replacement/revocation path first; do not blindly rotate JWT signing secret or invalidate users. Vercel OIDC requires checking owner-visible expiry/audience and any downstream exchange/session effects; a short-lived identity token is not assumed to be a manually rotatable API key. Update dependent secret stores/local files through an approved secure channel, validate with separately authorized minimal checks, invalidate old material and record metadata only. No provider credential validity test is needed to justify containment.
3. CI prevention, after explicit implementation/tool approval: pin a trusted scanner version/checksum with redaction verified using synthetic fixtures; no validity-verification network calls and no raw finding/SARIF/secret artifact uploads. Initial full reachable history plus current tree, PR changes, staged/new/renamed files and tracked env/key artifacts must be covered with fetch scope/skips attested. Fail new credentials and tracked private env files; reviewed false-positive exceptions must be narrow, owned and expire, with no blanket .env/high-entropy ignore or waiver for real exposed credentials. Add separate server/client env/import, DTO/log redaction and produced browser/server package scans tied to exact commit/build hash; do not claim present CI protection or run a build now.
4. History decision: rotating/revoking credentials is primary; history rewrite is useful for reducing discoverable material but cannot recall clones/forks/caches/artifacts. Current remote sharing is unknown, so no destructive rewrite/force-push is authorized or presumed necessary immediately. Decisions required for execution: (A) approve index-only untracking while preserving .env.local; (B) designate provider owner/environment and authorize exact containment/replacement actions, including Supabase legacy-key impact assessment; (C) approve pinned scanner installation/use and bounded CI implementation; (D) establish sharing/remote exposure metadata and decide coordinated history cleanup versus local-only handling.
